# Firstwish: lead's working list (rewritten 15 Nov ~15:10 UTC). THE BUILD HAS STARTED.

Owner ruling 15 Nov: everything so far is FRONT END ONLY ("screen built", never "done"). Now: design the admin dashboard and show it,
then build the whole backend (NestJS) and wire the front end. Workflow = the owner's skills: build-orchestration (the lead orchestrates,
writes no task code; cheap agents build, a different agent verifies; tiers T0/T1/T2; bundles; briefs 25 lines; 2 rounds max),
contract-driven-backend (Phases 0-8, artifacts in /home/user/Firstwish-API/.pipeline/), project-analysis (tasks.csv, cost, owner page),
frontend-surface-build, verification-harness, qa-audit. Re-read the skill (Skill tool: anthropic-skills:build-orchestration and
anthropic-skills:contract-driven-backend) after a context reset before spawning anything.

## Where things are
- API repo /home/user/Firstwish-API (branch claude/sleepy-mayer-f0hkb8, pushed): build/rulings.md (owner's words R1-R9),
  build/common-rules.md (40 lines, every agent reads it), build/tasks/ADM.md (admin scope, bundles A-D), build/prompts/*.md (briefs),
  build/ALLOC.md, .pipeline/framework-decision.json (NestJS, frozen), .pipeline/conventions.md, docs/product/*.txt (live documents, 15 Nov).
- Scratch for the build: scratchpad/build/{logs,verify,wt (worktrees),stacks}.
- Agents may NOT push (this session may push only claude/sleepy-mayer-f0hkb8 in each repo). They commit on a local branch in their own
  worktree; the lead merges (merge commit) into claude/sleepy-mayer-f0hkb8 and pushes. Merge often: a restart loses unpushed work.
- Models: EVERY agent on Sonnet (R17). Never Opus, never Fable. Machine: 4 CPUs, 15 GB, ~20 GB disk: at most 4 heavy agents.

## Next, in order
1. ADM-A hands back -> a separate verifier (lean, T1: claims, pictures at 360/390/1440, every drawn state, walk /app and /app/pro after
   /admin for class clashes) -> merge adm-a -> spawn ADM-B, ADM-C, ADM-D in parallel (briefs to write from build/tasks/ADM.md, each in
   its own worktree off the merged head, ports 3521/3531/3541) -> verify -> merge -> production build check (npm run build) -> the
   35-page walk (scratchpad/qa-walk/w.js + cmp.py; add the /admin pages) -> deploy -> SHOW THE OWNER the admin dashboard.
2. Detect + DocDigest hand back -> the lead writes .pipeline/declared/*.json (backend-only surfaces: Paystack + webhooks, SMS/WhatsApp/
   email ports, storage port with R2 and Spaces adapters, queue jobs for unlock/notice/rest/erase/reminders, ledger, upload screening,
   AI writing port, video render, QR card file, audit log, staff roles) and supersedes.json; then Phase 2: one extract agent per screen
   (read-only, spawn freely; include the admin screens once merged) -> Phase 3 reconcile (script) -> registry.json; conflicts go to the owner
   only in the six owner cases, else a recorded default.
3. project-analysis: tasks.csv from the registry + the inventory (scratchpad/tracker/rows.json, 225 rows, corrected), estimate.py, the
   owner page (visual: cost, time, plans, owner items).
4. Phase 4 schema + test kit + CI; Phase 5 build waves (money and identity first); Phase 6 wire; Phase 7 smoke; Phase 8 live gate.
